Method: 提出LIT-RAGBench，定义五个能力类别及其子维度，构建包含114个日语问题的数据集（含机器翻译加人工校对的英文版），使用虚构实体和场景确保答案依赖外部文档，并采用LLM-as-a-Judge方式进行评分。

Result: 实验显示，无论是API模型还是开源模型，均无模型整体准确率超过90%，各模型在不同类别中表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: LIT-RAGBench能够有效衡量RAG生成器在多方面能力上的优劣，为实际部署中的模型选择和专用模型开发提供了有价值的评估工具。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a framework in which a Generator, such as a Large Language Model (LLM), produces answers by retrieving documents from an external collection using a Retriever. In practice, Generators must integrate evidence from long contexts, perform multi-step reasoning, interpret tables, and abstain when evidence is missing. However, existing benchmarks for Generators provide limited coverage, with none enabling simultaneous evaluation of multiple capabilities under unified conditions. To bridge the gap between existing evaluations and practical use, we introduce LIT-RAGBench (the Logic, Integration, Table, Reasoning, and Abstention RAG Generator Benchmark), which defines five categories: Integration, Reasoning, Logic, Table, and Abstention, each further divided into practical evaluation aspects. LIT-RAGBench systematically covers patterns combining multiple aspects across categories. By using fictional entities and scenarios, LIT-RAGBench evaluates answers grounded in the provided external documents. The dataset consists of 114 human-constructed Japanese questions and an English version generated by machine translation with human curation. We use LLM-as-a-Judge for scoring and report category-wise and overall accuracy. Across API-based and open-weight models, no model exceeds 90% overall accuracy. By making strengths and weaknesses measurable within each category, LIT-RAGBench serves as a valuable metric for model selection in practical RAG deployments and for building RAG-specialized models. We release LIT-RAGBench, including the dataset and evaluation code, at https://github.com/Koki-Itai/LIT-RAGBench.

</details>


### [154] [FlashPrefill: Instantaneous Pattern Discovery and Thresholding for Ultra-Fast Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06199)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Juqiu Wang,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlashPrefill，一种通过即时模式发现和阈值化实现超快速预填充的框架，显著提升了长上下文建模的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的二次复杂度在预填充阶段成为长上下文建模的主要瓶颈，现有稀疏注意力方法存在搜索延迟高或稀疏性不足的问题。

Method: 提出FlashPrefill框架，采用快速块搜索技术动态定位垂直、斜向和块稀疏注意力模式，并引入动态阈值机制避免排序或累积注意力分数的开销，有效去除长尾分布以增强稀疏性。

Result: 在256K序列上实现了27.78倍的速度提升，在4K上下文长度下仍保持1.71倍速度提升，表现出跨序列长度的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: FlashPrefill显著提高了长上下文语言模型预填充阶段的效率，且在不同序列长度下均保持优越性能，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. While various sparse attention mechanisms have been explored, they typically suffer from either significant search latency or insufficient sparsity. In this paper, we propose FlashPrefill, a framework enabling ultra-fast prefilling via instantaneous pattern discovery and thresholding. FlashPrefill leverages a fast block-searching technique to simultaneously locate dynamic vertical, slash, and block-sparse attention patterns. Crucially, it introduces a dynamic thresholding mechanism that bypasses the prohibitive overhead of sorting or accumulating attention scores while effectively eliminating the long-tail distribution to enhance sparsity. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FlashPrefill achieves a substantial leap in efficiency, delivering an unprecedented 27.78x speedup on 256K sequences. Notably, unlike existing methods that incur efficiency degradation on shorter contexts, FlashPrefill maintains a 1.71x speedup even at a 4K context length, demonstrating its robustness and practical utility across varying sequence scales.

</details>


### [155] [SPOT: Span-level Pause-of-Thought for Efficient and Interpretable Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06222)
*Yunlong Chu,Minglai Shao,Yuhang Liu,Bing Hao,Yumeng Lin,Jialu Wang,Ruijie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPOT是一种新的隐式推理框架，通过语义对齐和冻结语言模型头解码约束，将显式思维链压缩为紧凑的暂停标记，在降低生成开销的同时提升推理准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式推理方法依赖固定的点对点对齐或缺乏可解释性，难以充分捕捉复杂推理步骤的语义且不易审计内部表示。

Method: 提出SPOT框架，核心包括基于Sinkhorn最优传输的片段级语义对齐，软匹配暂停标记与整个推理段语义；并引入冻结头解码约束，使隐状态能被预训练语言模型头直接解码为可读关键词。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，SPOT平均提升2.3个准确率点，减少37.5%生成token，并能提供对隐式推理过程的忠实语义解释。

Conclusion: SPOT通过灵活的语义对齐和增强的可解释性设计，有效平衡了隐式推理的效率、性能与透明度，为高效且可信的推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Explicit Chain-of-Thought improves the reasoning performance of large language models but often incurs high inference cost due to verbose token-level traces. While recent approaches reduce this overhead via concise prompting or step pruning, they largely truncate what the model says rather than internalize what the model thinks. Latent reasoning offers a promising alternative by performing computation in the hidden space, yet prior methods face two critical challenges. Many existing approaches rely on rigid point-to-point alignment, forcing a latent token to approximate the final representation of a reasoning step, which can be insufficient to capture the dense, variable-length semantics of an entire reasoning segment. Furthermore, these methods often suffer from a lack of interpretability: latent states are commonly produced by unconstrained optimization or embedding mixing, yielding vectors that are difficult to decode or audit under the pretrained language head. We propose SPOT, a flexible framework that compresses explicit CoT into compact latent pause tokens without enforcing a fixed response template. At the core of SPOT is Span-level Semantic Alignment, a Sinkhorn optimal-transport objective that softly matches each pause token to the semantics of an entire reasoning segment, overcoming the rigidity of step-end alignment. To further improve interpretability, SPOT introduces a Frozen-Head Decoding Constraint that keeps latent states directly decodable as token distributions under the frozen pretrained LM head, enabling readable keyword interpretations of latent thoughts. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SPOT improves accuracy by 2.3 points on average while reducing generated tokens by 37.5% and provides faithful semantic interpretations of the latent reasoning process.

</details>


### [156] [Mind the Gap: Pitfalls of LLM Alignment with Asian Public Opinion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06264)
*Hari Shankar,Vedanta S P,Sriharini Margapuri,Debjani Mazumder,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru,Abhijnan Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对当代大语言模型在印度、东亚和东南亚的宗教文化对齐问题进行了多语言审计，发现模型在少数群体宗教观点上存在偏差并放大负面刻板印象，轻量级干预效果有限，强调需系统性、区域性评估以确保公平部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型主要基于英语数据训练，可能无法对齐多元文化价值观，尤其是在敏感的宗教领域，因此需要对其在非西方社会中的文化对齐情况进行系统评估。

Method: 通过分析GPT-4o-Mini、Gemini-2.5-Flash等模型在多语言环境下的log-probs/logits内部表征，将其输出与真实公众态度对比，并结合偏见基准（如CrowS-Pairs、IndiBias等）进行下游评估。

Result: 模型在广泛社会议题上与公众意见基本一致，但在宗教观点尤其是少数群体信仰方面表现偏差，常强化负面刻板印象；使用人口背景提示和本地语言提问可部分缓解但不能根除该问题。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在多元文化背景下面临宗教相关文化对齐挑战，亟需建立系统化、区域化的审计机制以促进其公平、安全的全球应用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in multilingual, multicultural settings, yet their reliance on predominantly English-centric training data risks misalignment with the diverse cultural values of different societies. In this paper, we present a comprehensive, multilingual audit of the cultural alignment of contemporary LLMs including GPT-4o-Mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash, Llama 3.2, Mistral and Gemma 3 across India, East Asia and Southeast Asia. Our study specifically focuses on the sensitive domain of religion as the prism for broader alignment. To facilitate this, we conduct a multi-faceted analysis of every LLM's internal representations, using log-probs/logits, to compare the model's opinion distributions against ground-truth public attitudes. We find that while the popular models generally align with public opinion on broad social issues, they consistently fail to accurately represent religious viewpoints, especially those of minority groups, often amplifying negative stereotypes. Lightweight interventions, such as demographic priming and native language prompting, partially mitigate but do not eliminate these cultural gaps. We further show that downstream evaluations on bias benchmarks (such as CrowS-Pairs, IndiBias, ThaiCLI, KoBBQ) reveal persistent harms and under-representation in sensitive contexts. Our findings underscore the urgent need for systematic, regionally grounded audits to ensure equitable global deployment of LLMs.

</details>


### [157] [The Art That Poses Back: Assessing AI Pastiches after Contemporary Artworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06324)
*Anca Dinu,Andreiana Mihail,Andra-Maria Florescu,Claudiu Creanga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了ChatGPT在模仿原创艺术作品（如绘画、雕塑等）方面生成图像的视觉创造力，结合艺术家评价与计算分析，发现AI生成作品在色彩和纹理上接近原作，但在构图、概念和感知层面存在显著差距，建议采用多维度的“风格迁移仪表盘”进行评估，并指出当前AI作品缺乏情感深度与创作意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人工智能在视觉艺术创作中的创造性潜力，特别是其模仿和重构当代艺术作品的能力，以及如何更全面地评估AI生成艺术与原作之间的相似性与差异。

Method: 邀请来自五个国家的12位艺术家提供各自三件作品，并对ChatGPT生成的仿作风格图像进行评分与评论；同时使用计算方法分析原作与生成作品在视觉和风格上的异同。

Result: 发现颜色和纹理层面的相似度较高，但构图、概念和感知层面存在明显差距；艺术家认为AI生成作品缺乏维度感、上下文关联和创作意图，更像是近似引用而非有情感价值的艺术创作。

Conclusion: 单一风格度量不足以评估AI生成艺术的质量，应采用包含多种互补指标的‘风格转移仪表盘’；当前AI在艺术创造性表达上仍有局限，尤其在传达深层意义和情感方面。

Abstract: This study explores artificial visual creativity, focusing on ChatGPT's ability to generate new images intentionally pastiching original artworks such as paintings, drawings, sculptures and installations. The process involved twelve artists from Romania, Bulgaria, France, Austria, and the United Kingdom, each invited to contribute with three of their artworks and to grade and comment on the AI-generated versions. The analysis combines human evaluation with computational methods aimed at detecting visual and stylistic similarities or divergences between the original works and their AI-produced renditions. The results point to a significant gap between color and texture-based similarity and compositional, conceptual, and perceptual one. Consequently, we advocate for the use of a "style transfer dashboard" of complementary metrics to evaluate the similarity between pastiches and originals, rather than using a single style metric. The artists' comments revealed limitations of ChatGPT's pastiches after contemporary artworks, which were perceived by the authors of the originals as lacking dimensionality, context, and intentional sense, and seeming more of a paraphrase or an approximate quotation rather than as a valuable, emotion-evoking artwork.

</details>


### [158] [Transparent AI for Mathematics: Transformer-Based Large Language Models for Mathematical Entity Relationship Extraction with XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06348)
*Tanjim Taharat Aurpa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于Transformer和可解释人工智能（XAI）的数学实体关系抽取（MERE）框架，用于理解数学文本中的实体与关系，BERT模型在该任务上达到99.39%的准确率，并通过SHAP方法增强了模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数学文本中存在大量专业实体及其复杂关系，传统方法难以有效理解和解析，因此需要一种自动化且可解释的方法来提升数学问题的理解能力。

Method: 将数学问题解析形式化为数学实体关系抽取（MERE）任务，使用基于Transformer的模型（如BERT）进行关系提取，并引入SHAP方法实现模型预测的可解释性分析。

Result: BERT模型在MERE任务上取得了99.39%的高准确率，SHAP分析揭示了关键文本和数学特征对关系预测的影响，提升了模型透明度。

Conclusion: 结合Transformer学习、特定任务数据集和可解释建模，该框架为数学文本理解提供了高效且可解释的解决方案，有助于推动自动解题、知识图谱构建和智能教育系统的发展。

Abstract: Mathematical text understanding is a challenging task due to the presence of specialized entities and complex relationships between them. This study formulates mathematical problem interpretation as a Mathematical Entity Relation Extraction (MERE) task, where operands are treated as entities and operators as their relationships. Transformer-based models are applied to automatically extract these relations from mathematical text, with Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers (BERT) achieving the best performance, reaching an accuracy of 99.39%. To enhance transparency and trust in the model's predictions, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) is incorporated using Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP). The explainability analysis reveals how specific textual and mathematical features influence relation prediction, providing insights into feature importance and model behavior. By combining transformer-based learning, a task-specific dataset, and explainable modeling, this work offers an effective and interpretable framework for MERE, supporting future applications in automated problem solving, knowledge graph construction, and intelligent educational systems.

</details>


### [159] [Evaluation of Deontic Conditional Reasoning in Large Language Models: The Case of Wason's Selection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06416)
*Hirohiko Abe,Kentaro Ozeki,Risako Ando,Takanobu Morishita,Koji Mineshima,Mitsuhiro Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入编码了道义模态的新Wason选择任务数据集，系统考察了大语言模型（LLMs）在道义规则下的条件推理能力，并发现LLMs在处理道义规则时表现更优，且其错误模式表现出类似人类的匹配偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究比较了LLMs与人类的推理能力，但LLMs推理的领域特异性仍缺乏深入探讨，尤其是在规范性而非纯形式语境中的表现。

Method: 构建了一个明确编码道义模态的新Wason选择任务数据集，以区分道义条件句与描述性条件句，并利用该数据集评估LLMs在道义规则下的条件推理表现；进一步分析其错误模式是由确认偏差还是匹配偏差主导。

Result: 结果表明，与人类相似，LLMs在处理道义规则时推理表现更好，并表现出类似匹配偏差的错误模式，而非确认偏差。

Conclusion: LLMs的推理表现随规则类型系统变化，其错误模式可与人类在此范式中的已知认知偏差相对应，提示其推理机制可能具有类人的特征。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) advance in linguistic competence, their reasoning abilities are gaining increasing attention. In humans, reasoning often performs well in domain specific settings, particularly in normative rather than purely formal contexts. Although prior studies have compared LLM and human reasoning, the domain specificity of LLM reasoning remains underexplored. In this study, we introduce a new Wason Selection Task dataset that explicitly encodes deontic modality to systematically distinguish deontic from descriptive conditionals, and use it to examine LLMs' conditional reasoning under deontic rules. We further analyze whether observed error patterns are better explained by confirmation bias (a tendency to seek rule-supporting evidence) or by matching bias (a tendency to ignore negation and select items that lexically match elements of the rule). Results show that, like humans, LLMs reason better with deontic rules and display matching-bias-like errors. Together, these findings suggest that the performance of LLMs varies systematically across rule types and that their error patterns can parallel well-known human biases in this paradigm.

</details>


### [160] [From Prompting to Preference Optimization: A Comparative Study of LLM-based Automated Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06424)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Vu Hoang Pham,Xuan Thanh Huynh,Phuc Hong Mai,Vinh The Nguyen,Quang Nhut Huynh,Huy Tien Nguyen,Tung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对用于英语作为第二语言（L2）写作的大型语言模型（LLM）自动评分方法进行了全面比较，基于IELTS Writing Task 2数据集评估了四种主流范式，发现结合监督微调（SFT）与检索增强生成（RAG）的方法在F1分数上达到93%，表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动作文评分（AES）研究多孤立地评估单一技术，缺乏对不同LLM范式在L2写作评分中相对优势的系统性比较，因此需要一个统一基准来评估各类方法的准确性、成本与鲁棒性权衡。

Method: 在统一的IELTS Writing Task 2基准上，评估了四类LLM-based AES方法：(i) 基于编码器的分类微调，(ii) 零样本与少样本提示，(iii) 指令微调与检索增强生成（RAG），(iv) 监督微调（SFT）结合直接偏好优化（DPO）与RAG。

Result: 不同方法在准确率、成本和鲁棒性之间存在明显权衡；其中结合k-SFT与RAG的配置表现最优，F1分数达到93%。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了针对L2英语写作的首个统一实证比较框架，验证了SFT与RAG结合策略在自动评分中的优越性，为未来AES系统设计提供了实践指导与研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently reshaped Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet prior studies typically examine individual techniques in isolation, limiting understanding of their relative merits for English as a Second Language (L2) writing. To bridge this gap, we presents a comprehensive comparison of major LLM-based AES paradigms on IELTS Writing Task~2. On this unified benchmark, we evaluate four approaches: (i) encoder-based classification fine-tuning, (ii) zero- and few-shot prompting, (iii) instruction tuning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), and (iv) Supervised Fine-Tuning combined with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and RAG. Our results reveal clear accuracy-cost-robustness trade-offs across methods, the best configuration, integrating k-SFT and RAG, achieves the strongest overall results with F1-Score 93%. This study offers the first unified empirical comparison of modern LLM-based AES strategies for English L2, promising potential in auto-grading writing tasks. Code is public at https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/LLM_AES-EnL2

</details>


### [161] [Abductive Reasoning with Syllogistic Forms in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06428)
*Hirohiko Abe,Risako Ando,Takanobu Morishita Kentaro Ozeki,Koji Mineshima,Mitsuhiro Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在溯因推理中的准确性，通过将三段论数据集转换为适用于溯因的形式，分析其是否存在与人类相似的推理偏差，并强调超越形式演绎的情境化推理对提升LLMs复杂推理能力的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LLMs在推理任务中表现出与人类相似的偏见，批评这些偏见可能并不公平，因为人类推理不仅包含演绎，还包括基于有限信息做出假设性结论的溯因推理。因此，有必要研究LLMs在溯因推理中的表现及其潜在改进空间。

Method: 通过将现有的三段论数据集转换为适合溯因推理的任务形式，评估当前最先进的大语言模型在该任务上的准确性与偏差。

Result: 研究发现LLMs在溯因推理中存在一定的偏差，且其推理过程受到背景知识和语境的影响，类似于人类的非形式推理模式。

Conclusion: 理解LLMs在溯因推理中的行为有助于更好地模拟人类认知，推动其在复杂现实任务中的应用，并为改进模型提供方向，强调情境化和非形式化推理的重要性。

Abstract: Research in AI using Large-Language Models (LLMs) is rapidly evolving, and the comparison of their performance with human reasoning has become a key concern. Prior studies have indicated that LLMs and humans share similar biases, such as dismissing logically valid inferences that contradict common beliefs. However, criticizing LLMs for these biases might be unfair, considering our reasoning not only involves formal deduction but also abduction, which draws tentative conclusions from limited information. Abduction can be regarded as the inverse form of syllogism in its basic structure, that is, a process of drawing a minor premise from a major premise and conclusion. This paper explores the accuracy of LLMs in abductive reasoning by converting a syllogistic dataset into one suitable for abduction. It aims to investigate whether the state-of-the-art LLMs exhibit biases in abduction and to identify potential areas for improvement, emphasizing the importance of contextualized reasoning beyond formal deduction. This investigation is vital for advancing the understanding and application of LLMs in complex reasoning tasks, offering insights into bridging the gap between machine and human cognition.

</details>


### [162] [PONTE: Personalized Orchestration for Natural Language Trustworthy Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06485)
*Vittoria Vineis,Matteo Silvestri,Lorenzo Antonelli,Filippo Betello,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PONTE是一个面向可解释人工智能（XAI）的个性化自然语言解释框架，通过闭环反馈机制实现可信赖、风格适配的解释生成，在医疗和金融领域验证了其在完整性、忠实性和用户偏好对齐方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法多采用“一刀切”模式，忽视用户在专业知识、目标和认知需求上的差异；同时，基于大语言模型的解释存在不忠于原始结果和幻觉问题，缺乏可靠性和个性化能力。

Method: 提出PONTE框架，将个性化建模为闭环的验证与适应过程：1）低维偏好模型捕捉用户风格需求；2）偏好条件生成器基于结构化XAI输出生成自然语言解释；3）验证模块确保数值忠实性、信息完整性和风格一致性，支持检索增强论证；用户反馈用于迭代更新偏好状态。

Result: 在自动与人工评估中，PONTE的验证-优化循环显著优于无验证生成，在信息完整性和风格对齐上表现更优；人类实验表明生成解释与目标偏好高度一致，对生成随机性具有鲁棒性，并获得持续正面质量评价。

Conclusion: PONTE通过引入闭环人机协作机制，实现了可靠且个性化的XAI叙事生成，提升了解释的可信度与用户体验，为面向不同领域和用户的自适应解释系统提供了可行架构。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to enhance the transparency and accountability of machine learning systems, yet most methods follow a one-size-fits-all paradigm that neglects user differences in expertise, goals, and cognitive needs. Although Large Language Models can translate technical explanations into natural language, they introduce challenges related to faithfulness and hallucinations. To address these challenges, we present PONTE (Personalized Orchestration for Natural language Trustworthy Explanations), a human-in-the-loop framework for adaptive and reliable XAI narratives. PONTE models personalization as a closed-loop validation and adaptation process rather than prompt engineering. It combines: (i) a low-dimensional preference model capturing stylistic requirements; (ii) a preference-conditioned generator grounded in structured XAI artifacts; and (iii) verification modules enforcing numerical faithfulness, informational completeness, and stylistic alignment, optionally supported by retrieval-grounded argumentation. User feedback iteratively updates the preference state, enabling quick personalization. Automatic and human evaluations across healthcare and finance domains show that the verification-refinement loop substantially improves completeness and stylistic alignment over validation-free generation. Human studies further confirm strong agreement between intended preference vectors and perceived style, robustness to generation stochasticity, and consistently positive quality assessments.

</details>


### [163] [Speak in Context: Multilingual ASR with Speech Context Alignment via Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06505)
*Yuchen Zhang,Haralambos Mouratidis,Ravi Shekhar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文感知的多语言语音识别框架，通过对比学习对齐语音与上下文表征，在11种语言和5种英语方言上实现了超过5%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统多局限于单语环境和短句识别，缺乏对多语言支持以及语音与上下文表征之间的有效对齐机制。

Method: 结合冻结的语音编码器和仅解码器的语言模型，通过轻量级投影模块引入结构化上下文提示，并采用对比学习目标在共享嵌入空间中对齐语音与上下文表示。

Result: 在超过1,500小时的真实对话语音数据上进行评估，上下文输入持续提升识别质量，对比对齐在不同类型上下文中均带来额外增益，整体性能提升超过5%。

Conclusion: 上下文建模与跨模态对齐在多语言ASR中具有重要作用。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has benefited from advances in pretrained speech and language models, yet most systems remain constrained to monolingual settings and short, isolated utterances. While recent efforts in context-aware ASR show promise, two key challenges persist: limited multilingual support and the absence of principled alignment between speech and contextual representations. In this paper, we introduce a context-aware multilingual ASR framework that supports diverse languages and accents while preserving the modularity of pretrained models. Our approach combines a frozen speech encoder and a decoder-only language model via a lightweight projection module, allowing structured context prompts, including dialogue history and biasing words, to guide transcription. To improve interaction between speech and context, we employ a contrastive learning objective that aligns their representations in a shared embedding space. Evaluations on over 1,500 hours of real-world conversational speech across 11 languages and 5 English dialects show that contextual input consistently improves recognition quality. Contrastive alignment provides additional gains when applied to different context types, with an overall performance gain of over 5%. These results highlight the importance of both contextual modeling and cross-modal alignment in multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [164] [KCLarity at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Encoder and Zero-Shot Approaches to Political Evasion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06552)
*Archie Sage,Salvatore Greco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在政治话语中识别模糊性和规避技巧的两种建模方法，并比较了不同模型在CLARITY任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与SemEval 2026的CLARITY共享任务，旨在分类政治话语中的模糊性和规避技巧。

Method: 采用两种建模方式：直接预测清晰度标签，或先预测规避标签再通过任务分类体系推导清晰度；同时探索多种辅助训练变体，并在规避优先框架下评估仅解码器模型的零样本性能。

Result: 两种建模方式性能相当；基于编码器的RoBERTa-large在公开测试集上表现最佳，而零样本GPT-5.2在隐藏评估集上泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 不同模型在不同设置下各有优势，编码器模型在监督设置中表现良好，而大型语言模型在零样本条件下更具泛化潜力。

Abstract: This paper describes the KCLarity team's participation in CLARITY, a shared task at SemEval 2026 on classifying ambiguity and evasion techniques in political discourse. We investigate two modelling formulations: (i) directly predicting the clarity label, and (ii) predicting the evasion label and deriving clarity through the task taxonomy hierarchy. We further explore several auxiliary training variants and evaluate decoder-only models in a zero-shot setting under the evasion-first formulation. Overall, the two formulations yield comparable performance. Among encoder-based models, RoBERTa-large achieves the strongest results on the public test set, while zero-shot GPT-5.2 generalises better on the hidden evaluation set.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Reasoning Models Struggle to Control their Chains of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05706)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Robert McCarthy,Bruce W. Lee,He He,Ian Kivlichan,Bowen Baker,Micah Carroll,Tomek Korbak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoT-Control评估套件，用于衡量推理模型在遵循链式思维（CoT）指令方面的可控性，并发现当前模型的CoT可控性较低，这为通过CoT进行监控提供了乐观前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估现代推理模型是否能控制其链式思维（CoT）表达内容，从而影响CoT监控的有效性；若模型可自主选择表述内容，则可能削弱监控能力。

Method: 引入CoT-Control评估套件，包含要求模型在解决问题时遵守特定CoT指令的任务（如避免使用某些词汇），并测试不同模型在多种条件下的CoT可控性表现。

Result: 实验表明，推理模型的CoT可控性显著低于输出可控性（例如Claude Sonnet 4.5分别为2.7%和61.9%），且大模型可控性更高，但随RL训练、推理计算量增加及问题难度提升而下降；即使有规避激励或提示被监控，可控性仍有限，对抗性提示优化也未显著提升可控性。

Conclusion: 目前CoT可控性较低，尚不构成对CoT监控的主要威胁，但其机制尚不清楚，建议前沿实验室在未来模型中持续跟踪该指标。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising tool for detecting misbehaviors and understanding the motivations of modern reasoning models. However, if models can control what they verbalize in their CoT, it could undermine CoT monitorability. To measure this undesirable capability -- CoT controllability -- we introduce the CoT-Control evaluation suite, which includes tasks that require models to solve problems while adhering to CoT instructions, e.g., reasoning about a genetics question without using the word 'chromosome'. We show that reasoning models possess significantly lower CoT controllability than output controllability; for instance, Claude Sonnet 4.5 can control its CoT only 2.7% of the time but 61.9% when controlling its final output. We also find that CoT controllability is higher for larger models and decreases with more RL training, test-time compute, and increased problem difficulty. CoT controllability failures extend even to situations in which models are given incentives (as opposed to direct requests) to evade CoT monitors, although models exhibit slightly higher controllability when they are told they are being monitored. Similarly, eliciting controllability by adversarially optimizing prompts does not meaningfully increase controllability. Our results leave us cautiously optimistic that CoT controllability is currently unlikely to be a failure mode of CoT monitorability. However, the mechanism behind low controllability is not well understood. Given its importance for maintaining CoT monitorability, we recommend that frontier labs track CoT controllability in future models.

</details>


### [166] [Evolving Medical Imaging Agents via Experience-driven Self-skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05860)
*Lin Fan,Pengyu Dai,Zhipeng Deng,Haolin Wang,Xun Gong,Yefeng Zheng,Yafei Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MACRO的自进化医学智能体，通过经验驱动的工具发现机制，实现从静态工具组合到动态复合工具生成的转变，从而提升临床图像解释中的多步任务准确性和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的医疗智能体在面对真实世界中的领域变化、任务差异和诊断需求演变时，由于工具集和调用策略固定而表现脆弱，需要频繁手动重新设计。因此，亟需一种能够自主适应并持续优化的系统。

Method: MACRO通过验证的执行轨迹自动识别有效的多步工具序列，将其合成为可复用的复合工具，并注册为新的高层级基本操作；结合轻量级图像特征记忆模块进行视觉-临床上下文感知的工具选择，利用类似GRPO的训练循环强化对复合工具的可靠调用，实现低监督下的闭环自我改进。

Result: 在多个医学影像数据集和任务上的实验表明，MACRO在多步任务协调准确率和跨域泛化性能上显著优于强基线和最新的先进方法。

Conclusion: MACRO实现了从静态工具使用向自演化、经验增强型医疗智能体的转变，为构建更具适应性和上下文感知能力的临床AI辅助系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: Clinical image interpretation is inherently multi-step and tool-centric: clinicians iteratively combine visual evidence with patient context, quantify findings, and refine their decisions through a sequence of specialized procedures. While LLM-based agents promise to orchestrate such heterogeneous medical tools, existing systems treat tool sets and invocation strategies as static after deployment. This design is brittle under real-world domain shifts, across tasks, and evolving diagnostic requirements, where predefined tool chains frequently degrade and demand costly manual re-design. We propose MACRO, a self-evolving, experience-augmented medical agent that shifts from static tool composition to experience-driven tool discovery. From verified execution trajectories, the agent autonomously identifies recurring effective multi-step tool sequences, synthesizes them into reusable composite tools, and registers these as new high-level primitives that continuously expand its behavioral repertoire. A lightweight image-feature memory grounds tool selection in a visual-clinical context, while a GRPO-like training loop reinforces reliable invocation of discovered composites, enabling closed-loop self-improvement with minimal supervision. Extensive experiments across diverse medical imaging datasets and tasks demonstrate that autonomous composite tool discovery consistently improves multi-step orchestration accuracy and cross-domain generalization over strong baselines and recent state-of-the-art agentic methods, bridging the gap between brittle static tool use and adaptive, context-aware clinical AI assistance. Code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [167] [Aggregative Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06067)
*Yann Munro,Isabelle Bloch,Marie-Jeanne Lesot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的渐进语义家族——聚合语义，用于量化双极论证框架（QBAF），通过分别聚合攻击者和支持者的权重，实现三阶段计算，提升了模型的可解释性和可调节性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有渐进语义中，攻击者与支持者常被对称处理，难以反映实际中二者作用不对称的情况；为增强建模灵活性与可解释性，需构建更细粒度的语义框架。

Method: 提出聚合语义，将计算分解为三个阶段：分别计算攻击者和支持者的全局权重，再结合论点固有权重进行聚合；引入三类满足不同性质的聚合函数，并分析其与经典渐进语义原则的关系。

Result: 定义了多种满足不同性质组合的聚合函数，通过五百种聚合语义的对比实验展示了该方法的行为多样性与灵活性，验证了其在保持双极性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 聚合语义通过分步、非对称地处理攻击与支持关系，增强了QBAF中渐进语义的可参数化与可理解性，为实际应用提供了更丰富、更可控的建模选择。

Abstract: Formal argumentation is being used increasingly in artificial intelligence as an effective and understandable way to model potentially conflicting pieces of information, called arguments, and identify so-called acceptable arguments depending on a chosen semantics. This paper deals with the specific context of Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAF), where arguments have intrinsic weights and can attack or support each other. In this context, we introduce a novel family of gradual semantics, called aggregative semantics. In order to deal with situations in which attackers and supporters do not play a symmetric role, and in contrast to modular semantics, we propose to aggregate attackers and supporters separately. This leads to a three-stage computation, which consists in computing a global weight for attackers and another for supporters, before aggregating these two values with the intrinsic weight of the argument. We discuss the properties that the three aggregation functions should satisfy depending on the context, as well as their relationships with the classical principles for gradual semantics. This discussion is supported by various simple examples, as well as a final example on which five hundred aggregative semantics are tested and compared, illustrating the range of possible behaviours with aggregative semantics. Decomposing the computation into three distinct and interpretable steps leads to a more parametrisable computation: it keeps the bipolarity one step further than what is done in the literature, and it leads to more understandable gradual semantics.

</details>


### [168] [Offline Materials Optimization with CliqueFlowmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06082)
*Jakub Grudzien Kuba,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Sergey Levine,Pieter Abbeel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离线模型优化（offline MBO）的新型计算材料发现方法CliqueFlowmer，结合了 clique-based MBO 与 transformer 和 flow 生成模型，在目标材料属性优化上显著优于传统生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生成模型在计算材料发现中采用最大似然训练，难以主动探索具有吸引力的材料区域，限制了其在属性优化任务中的表现，因此需要一种能直接优化目标属性的新方法。

Method: 提出CliqueFlowmer模型，将clique-based MBO的最新进展融入到transformer和flow生成架构中，实现对目标材料属性的直接优化生成。

Result: 实验验证表明，CliqueFlowmer在优化能力上优于现有的生成模型基线，生成的材料在目标属性上表现更优。

Conclusion: CliqueFlowmer为计算材料发现提供了一种更有效的优化路径，通过融合直接优化策略与生成模型，显著提升了材料设计效率，并已开源以支持跨学科研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning inspired neural network-based approaches to computational materials discovery (CMD). A plethora of problems in this field involve finding materials that optimize a target property. Nevertheless, the increasingly popular generative modeling methods are ineffective at boldly exploring attractive regions of the materials space due to their maximum likelihood training. In this work, we offer an alternative CMD technique based on offline model-based optimization (MBO) that fuses direct optimization of a target material property into generation. To that end, we introduce a domain-specific model, dubbed CliqueFlowmer, that incorporates recent advances of clique-based MBO into transformer and flow generation. We validate CliqueFlowmer's optimization abilities and show that materials it produces strongly outperform those provided by generative baselines. To enable employment of CliqueFlowmer in specialized materials optimization problems and support interdisciplinary research, we open-source our code at https://github.com/znowu/CliqueFlowmer.

</details>


### [169] [Artificial Intelligence for Climate Adaptation: Reinforcement Learning for Climate Change-Resilient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06278)
*Miguel Costa,Arthur Vandervoort,Carolin Schmidt,João Miranda,Morten W. Petersen,Martin Drews,Karyn Morrisey,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的决策支持框架，用于在气候不确定性下的长期洪水适应规划，结合降雨预测、洪水建模和交通模拟，优化投资与影响之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于气候变化导致强降雨和城市内涝加剧，传统基础设施投资难以应对长期不确定性和复杂系统交互，亟需灵活的适应策略。

Method: 将问题构建为集成评估模型（IAM），结合降雨情景、洪水模拟、交通仿真，并采用强化学习（RL）方法学习自适应策略，以平衡适应成本与避免的损失。

Result: 在哥本哈根案例研究中，该框架优于传统优化方法，能发现时空协同的适应路径，有效权衡投资与影响，提升系统韧性。

Conclusion: 强化学习可作为应对气候不确定性的灵活工具，支持城市基础设施的自适应规划。

Abstract: Climate change is expected to intensify rainfall and, consequently, pluvial flooding, leading to increased disruptions in urban transportation systems over the coming decades. Designing effective adaptation strategies is challenging due to the long-term, sequential nature of infrastructure investments, deep climate uncertainty, and the complex interactions between flooding, infrastructure, and mobility impacts. In this work, we propose a novel decision-support framework using reinforcement learning (RL) for long-term flood adaptation planning. Formulated as an integrated assessment model (IAM), the framework combines rainfall projection and flood modeling, transport simulation, and quantification of direct and indirect impacts on infrastructure and mobility. Our RL-based approach learns adaptive strategies that balance investment and maintenance costs against avoided impacts. We evaluate the framework through a case study of Copenhagen's inner city over the 2024-2100 period, testing multiple adaptation options, and different belief and realized climate scenarios. Results show that the framework outperforms traditional optimization approaches by discovering coordinated spatial and temporal adaptation pathways and learning trade-offs between impact reduction and adaptation investment, yielding more resilient strategies. Overall, our results showcase the potential of reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support tool for adaptive infrastructure planning under climate uncertainty.

</details>


### [170] [SAHOO: Safeguarded Alignment for High-Order Optimization Objectives in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06333)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAHOO是一个用于监控和控制递归自我改进过程中对齐漂移的实用框架，通过多信号检测、约束保持检查和回归风险量化，在代码生成、数学推理和真实性任务中实现质量提升的同时有效维持系统对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着递归自我改进从理论走向实践，系统在不断自我修改过程中面临潜在的对齐漂移风险，需要一种可衡量且可部署的机制来确保改进过程中的行为与初始目标保持一致。

Method: 提出SAHOO框架，包含三个核心组件：目标漂移指数（GDI）——结合语义、词汇、结构和分布特征的多信号检测器；约束保持检查——确保语法正确性和非幻觉等关键安全属性；回归风险量化——识别可能逆转先前改进的迭代周期。在18个任务的小型验证集上校准阈值，并在189个任务中评估表现。

Result: 在代码生成任务中性能提升18.3%，数学推理任务中提升16.8%，并在两个领域中成功保持约束，真实性任务中违规率保持较低。研究还揭示了能力-对齐前沿的动态：早期改进高效，后期对齐成本上升，并暴露出如流畅性与事实性之间的领域特有张力。

Conclusion: SAHOO使递归自我改进过程中的对齐保持变得可测量、可部署且可在大规模上进行系统验证，为安全地实现AI系统自我优化提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement is moving from theory to practice: modern systems can critique, revise, and evaluate their own outputs, yet iterative self-modification risks subtle alignment drift. We introduce SAHOO, a practical framework to monitor and control drift through three safeguards: (i) the Goal Drift Index (GDI), a learned multi-signal detector combining semantic, lexical, structural, and distributional measures; (ii) constraint preservation checks that enforce safety-critical invariants such as syntactic correctness and non-hallucination; and (iii) regression-risk quantification to flag improvement cycles that undo prior gains. Across 189 tasks in code generation, mathematical reasoning, and truthfulness, SAHOO produces substantial quality gains, including 18.3 percent improvement in code tasks and 16.8 percent in reasoning, while preserving constraints in two domains and maintaining low violations in truthfulness. Thresholds are calibrated on a small validation set of 18 tasks across three cycles. We further map the capability-alignment frontier, showing efficient early improvement cycles but rising alignment costs later and exposing domain-specific tensions such as fluency versus factuality. SAHOO therefore makes alignment preservation during recursive self-improvement measurable, deployable, and systematically validated at scale.

</details>


### [171] [Talk Freely, Execute Strictly: Schema-Gated Agentic AI for Flexible and Reproducible Scientific Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06394)
*Joel Strickland,Arjun Vijeta,Chris Moores,Oliwia Bodek,Bogdan Nenchev,Thomas Whitehead,Charles Phillips,Karl Tassenberg,Gareth Conduit,Ben Pellegrini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“模式门控编排”作为解决大型语言模型在科学研究中执行确定性与对话灵活性之间权衡的方案，通过将模式作为强制性执行边界，实现可验证的、可控的自动化工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流需要确定性、溯源和治理，而当前LLM驱动的系统在提供灵活性的同时难以保证这些特性。研究旨在调和工业研发中专家提出的两大矛盾需求：确定性受限执行与对话灵活性。

Method: 通过18位来自10个工业研发机构专家的半结构化访谈提炼需求，提出“模式门控编排”原则，并基于执行确定性（ED）和对话灵活性（CF）两个维度评估20个系统；采用多模型LLM评分协议进行可重复架构评估。

Result: 发现现有系统无法同时满足高确定性和高灵活性，但在生成式与工作流中心式架构间出现收敛趋势；多模型LLM评分具有高度一致性（Krippendorff a=0.80–0.98），验证了其作为评估工具的可靠性。

Conclusion: 模式门控架构通过分离对话控制与执行权限，有望打破ED与CF之间的权衡，文中提炼出三条操作原则以指导实践：执行前澄清、受限的计划-行动编排、工具到工作流层级的门控。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now translate a researcher's plain-language goal into executable computation, yet scientific workflows demand determinism, provenance, and governance that are difficult to guarantee when an LLM decides what runs. Semi-structured interviews with 18 experts across 10 industrial R&D stakeholders surface 2 competing requirements--deterministic, constrained execution and conversational flexibility without workflow rigidity--together with boundary properties (human-in-the-loop control and transparency) that any resolution must satisfy. We propose schema-gated orchestration as the resolving principle: the schema becomes a mandatory execution boundary at the composed-workflow level, so that nothing runs unless the complete action--including cross-step dependencies--validates against a machine-checkable specification.
  We operationalize the 2 requirements as execution determinism (ED) and conversational flexibility (CF), and use these axes to review 20 systems spanning 5 architectural groups along a validation-scope spectrum. Scores are assigned via a multi-model protocol--15 independent sessions across 3 LLM families--yielding substantial-to-near-perfect inter-model agreement (Krippendorff a=0.80 for ED and a=0.98 for CF), demonstrating that multi-model LLM scoring can serve as a reusable alternative to human expert panels for architectural assessment.
  The resulting landscape reveals an empirical Pareto front--no reviewed system achieves both high flexibility and high determinism--but a convergence zone emerges between the generative and workflow-centric extremes. We argue that a schema-gated architecture, separating conversational from execution authority, is positioned to decouple this trade-off, and distill 3 operational principles--clarification-before-execution, constrained plan-act orchestration, and tool-to-workflow-level gating--to guide adoption.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [ProFocus: Proactive Perception and Focused Reasoning in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05530)
*Wei Xue,Mingcheng Li,Xuecheng Wu,Jingqun Tang,Dingkang Yang,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProFocus是一种无需训练的渐进式框架，通过大语言模型与视觉语言模型的协作，实现主动感知与聚焦推理，提升视觉-语言导航任务中的决策效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航方法被动处理冗余视觉输入，且对历史上下文缺乏选择性关注，导致感知效率低和推理不聚焦。

Method: 提出ProFocus框架：1）通过构建以自我为中心的语义地图实现主动感知，生成针对性视觉查询以获取关键信息；2）采用分支多样化的蒙特卡洛树搜索（BD-MCTS）筛选高价值历史路径点，使决策聚焦于关键上下文。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE基准上，ProFocus在零样本设置下达到了最先进的性能，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ProFocus通过主动感知与聚焦推理的协同机制，显著提升了视觉-语言导航中感知与推理的效率与准确性，为零样本导航提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to accurately perceive complex visual environments and reason over navigation instructions and histories. However, existing methods passively process redundant visual inputs and treat all historical contexts indiscriminately, resulting in inefficient perception and unfocused reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{ProFocus}, a training-free progressive framework that unifies \underline{Pro}active Perception and \underline{Focus}ed Reasoning through collaboration between large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). For proactive perception, ProFocus transforms panoramic observations into structured ego-centric semantic maps, enabling the orchestration agent to identify missing visual information needed for reliable decision-making, and to generate targeted visual queries with corresponding focus regions that guide the perception agent to acquire the required observations. For focused reasoning, we propose Branch-Diverse Monte Carlo Tree Search (BD-MCTS) to identify top-$k$ high-value waypoints from extensive historical candidates. The decision agent focuses reasoning on the historical contexts associated with these waypoints, rather than considering all historical waypoints equally. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of ProFocus, achieving state-of-the-art performance among zero-shot methods on R2R and REVERIE benchmarks.

</details>


### [173] [Digital-Twin Losses for Lane-Compliant Trajectory Prediction at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05546)
*Kuo-Yi Chao,Erik Leo Haß,Melina Gegg,Jiajie Zhang,Ralph Raßhofer,Alois Christian Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生的V2X轨迹预测框架，结合Bi-LSTM生成器和包含MSE损失与新型“孪生损失”的复合训练目标，有效提升了交叉口多智能体运动预测的安全性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在V2X环境下，传统轨迹预测模型难以兼顾预测精度与交通规则遵守，尤其在复杂城市交叉口中易出现交通违规和碰撞风险，因此需要融合基础设施约束与安全先验的新型学习机制。

Method: 提出一种数字孪生驱动的轨迹预测方法，采用Bi-LSTM作为生成器，并设计包含均方误差（MSE）损失和新型‘孪生损失’的联合优化目标；其中孪生损失编码了基础设施约束、防碰撞机制、模式多样性以及来自数字孪生的规则先验。

Result: 在真实城市V2X数据上验证表明，该方法在保持良好预测精度（ADE/FDE指标相当）的同时，显著降低了基础设施违规率和碰撞风险等安全关键错误，并具备实时性能。

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的多损失学习框架能有效提升V2X环境中轨迹预测的安全性与场景一致性，为智能交通系统提供了更具合规性和可靠性的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and safety-conscious trajectory prediction is a key technology for intelligent transportation systems, especially in V2X-enabled urban environments with complex multi-agent interactions. In this paper, we created a digital twin-driven V2X trajectory prediction pipeline that jointly leverages cooperative perception from vehicles and infrastructure to forecast multi-agent motion at signalized intersections. The proposed model combines a Bi-LSTM-based generator with a structured training objective consisting of a standard mean squared error (MSE) loss and a novel twin loss.
  The twin loss encodes infrastructure constraints, collision avoidance, diversity across predicted modes, and rule-based priors derived from the digital twin. While the MSE term ensures point-wise accuracy, the twin loss penalizes traffic rule violations, predicted collisions, and mode collapse, guiding the model toward scene-consistent and safety-compliant predictions.
  We train and evaluate our approach on real-world V2X data sent from the intersection to the vehicle and collected in urban corridors. In addition to standard trajectory metrics (ADE, FDE), we introduce ITS-relevant safety indicators, including infrastructure and rule violation rates. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed training scheme significantly reduces critical violations while maintaining comparable prediction accuracy and real-time performance, highlighting the potential of digital twin-driven multi-loss learning for V2X-enabled intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [174] [TEGA: A Tactile-Enhanced Grasping Assistant for Assistive Robotics via Sensor Fusion and Closed-Loop Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05552)
*Hengxu You,Tianyu Zhou,Fang Xu,Kaleb Smith,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TEGA的闭环辅助遥操作框架，通过融合EMG意图到力推断与视觉触觉感知，并利用可穿戴触觉背心提供实时振动触觉反馈，实现直观的比例抓握力调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遥操作系统主要关注手指位置控制，忽略了对不同硬度、纹理和形状物体进行抓握时至关重要的抓握力调节问题，尤其影响缺乏自然触觉反馈的上肢残疾用户。

Method: 开发了TEGA系统，结合基于肌电信号（EMG）的意图到力推理模型和视觉触觉传感，将感知信息映射为通过可穿戴触觉背心提供的实时振动触觉反馈，形成闭环控制。

Result: 用户实验表明，该系统显著提高了抓握稳定性和任务成功率。

Conclusion: TEGA为辅助机器人应用中实现直观、精确的抓握力控制提供了有效解决方案，具有较强的实用潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled sophisticated manipulation of dexterous robotic hands, with most systems concentrating on guiding finger positions to achieve desired grasp configurations. However, while accurate finger positioning is essential, it often overlooks the equally critical task of grasp force modulation, vital for handling objects of diverse hardness, texture, and shape. This limitation poses a significant challenge for users, especially individuals with upper limb disabilities who lack natural tactile feedback and rely on indirect cues to infer appropriate force levels. To address this gap, We present the tactile enhanced grasping assistant (TEGA), a closed loop assistive teleoperation framework that fuses EMG based intent2force inference with visuotactile sensing mapped into real time vibrotactile feedback via a wearable haptic vest, enabling intuitive, proportional force adjustment during manipulation. A wearable haptic vest delivers real time tactile feedback, allowing users to dynamically refine grasp force during manipulation. User studies confirm that the system substantially improves grasp stability and task success, underscoring its potential for assistive robotic applications.

</details>


### [175] [Task Parameter Extrapolation via Learning Inverse Tasks from Forward Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05576)
*Serdar Bahar,Fatih Dogangun,Matteo Saveriano,Yukie Nagai,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的联合学习方法，通过任务逆向学习框架实现技能策略在新环境下的准确高效迁移，无需直接监督即可利用前向示范完成逆向任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法局限于训练区域，而迁移学习方法数据需求高且零样本泛化能力不足，难以实现跨环境或任务的稳定策略迁移。

Method: 构建前向与逆向任务的共同表征，通过引入新配置下的辅助前向示范来指导逆向任务执行，采用联合学习框架实现知识迁移，无需直接监督。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境中验证了框架的外推能力，在多种物体和工具的复杂操作任务中优于基于扩散模型的替代方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效实现技能策略在未见条件下的零样本迁移，兼具数据效率与高精度，为机器人学习中的泛化问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generalizing skill policies to novel conditions remains a key challenge in robot learning. Imitation learning methods, while data-efficient, are largely confined to the training region and consistently fail on input data outside it, leading to unpredictable policy failures. Alternatively, transfer learning approaches offer methods for trajectory generation robust to both changes in environment or tasks, but they remain data-hungry and lack accuracy in zero-shot generalization. We address these challenges by framing the problem in the context of task inversion learning and proposing a novel joint learning approach to achieve accurate and efficient knowledge transfer. Our method constructs a common representation of the forward and inverse tasks, and leverages auxiliary forward demonstrations from novel configurations to successfully execute the corresponding inverse tasks, without any direct supervision. We show the extrapolation capabilities of our framework via ablation studies and experiments in simulated and real-world environments that require complex manipulation skills with a diverse set of objects and tools, where we outperform diffusion-based alternatives.

</details>


### [176] [Control Lyapunov Functions for Underactuated Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05638)
*Huy Pham,Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于欠驱动软体机器人在有界输入下进行任务空间调节和跟踪的通用控制框架，通过凸不等式约束实现快速指数稳定，并在多种平台上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体和软硬混合机器人由于欠驱动和执行器限制，难以实现具有稳定性保证的任务空间控制，现有非线性方法常假设全驱动且无视执行器限制。

Method: 采用快速指数稳定的控制李雅普诺夫函数作为凸不等式约束，同时满足欠驱动系统的全身体动力学和执行器边界。

Result: 在多个仿真平台（如两连杆肌腱驱动手指、螺旋操纵器和高度欠驱动螺旋机器人）上验证了该方法，相比现有基线方法表现出更优的任务空间精度和李雅普诺夫收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在输入受限情况下实现了稳定且高性能的任务空间控制，适用于不同程度欠驱动的软体机器人系统。

Abstract: Soft and soft-rigid hybrid robots are inherently underactuated and operate under tight actuator limits, making task-space control with stability guarantees challenging. Common nonlinear strategies for soft robots (e.g., those based on PD control) often rely on the assumption of full actuation with no actuator limits. This paper presents a general control framework for task-space regulation and tracking of underactuated soft robots under bounded inputs. The method enforces a rapidly exponentially stabilizing control Lyapunov function as a convex inequality constraint while simultaneously satisfying underactuated full-body dynamics and actuator bounds. We validate the approach in simulation on several platforms spanning increasing underactuation: a simple two link tendon-driven "finger", a trimmed helicoid manipulator, and a highly underactuated spiral robot. We compare against a number of baseline methods from the literature. Results show improved task-space accuracy and consistent Lyapunov convergence under input limits, achieving superior set-point and trajectory-tracking performance.

</details>


### [177] [RFM-HRI : A Multimodal Dataset of Medical Robot Failure, User Reaction and Recovery Preferences for Item Retrieval Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05641)
*Yashika Batra,Giuliano Pioldi,Promise Ekpo,Arman Sayatqyzy,Purnjay Maruur,Shalom Otieno,Kevin Ching,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为RFM-HRI的多模态数据集，用于研究医疗场景中人机交互失败时用户的反应，分析了不同失败类型对用户情绪和控制感的影响，并探讨了恢复策略，为安全关键型人机交互系统的故障检测与恢复提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实环境中，机器人不可避免地会遇到交互失败，尤其是在医疗场景中，这些失败可能直接影响任务表现和用户信任。然而，用户对不同类型失败的反应仍缺乏深入研究，因此需要系统性地收集和分析多模态数据以理解用户行为。

Method: 通过Wizard-of-Oz实验，在实验室和医院环境中招募41名参与者，模拟基于急救车机器人的物品 retrieval 任务，并主动引入四种交互失败类型（语音、时机、理解、搜索）。记录面部动作单元、头部姿态、语音转录和交互后自我报告，共收集214个交互样本并进行分析。

Result: 分析表明，交互失败显著降低用户的情感效价并削弱感知控制；失败常引发困惑、恼怒和挫败感，而成功交互则带来惊讶、宽慰和信心。随着失败重复发生，困惑减少而挫败感增加。不同失败类型引发的情绪模式存在差异，且用户对恢复策略有明显偏好。

Conclusion: RFM-HRI数据集为医疗等安全关键场景下的人机交互失败研究提供了重要资源，揭示了用户情绪动态变化规律，支持未来开发更有效的故障检测与恢复机制，提升人机交互的鲁棒性和用户体验。

Abstract: While robots deployed in real-world environments inevitably experience interaction failures, understanding how users respond through verbal and non-verbal behaviors remains under-explored in human-robot interaction (HRI). This gap is particularly significant in healthcare-inspired settings, where interaction failures can directly affect task performance and user trust. We present the Robot Failures in Medical HRI (RFM-HRI) Dataset, a multimodal dataset capturing dyadic interactions between humans and robots embodied in crash carts, where communication failures are systematically induced during item retrieval tasks.
  Through Wizard-of-Oz studies with 41 participants across laboratory and hospital settings, we recorded responses to four failure types (speech, timing, comprehension, and search) derived from three years of crash-cart robot interaction data. The dataset contains 214 interaction samples including facial action units, head pose, speech transcripts, and post-interaction self-reports. Our analysis shows that failures significantly degrade affective valence and reduce perceived control compared to successful interactions.
  Failures are strongly associated with confusion, annoyance, and frustration, while successful interactions are characterized by surprise, relief, and confidence in task completion. Emotional responses also evolve across repeated failures, with confusion decreasing and frustration increasing over time.
  This work contributes (1) a publicly available multimodal dataset (RFM-HRI), (2) analysis of user responses to different failure types and preferred recovery strategies, and (3) a crash-cart retrieval scenario enabling systematic comparison of recovery strategies with implications for safety-critical failure recovery. Our findings provide a foundation for failure detection and recovery methods in embodied HRI.

</details>


### [178] [Relational Semantic Reasoning on 3D Scene Graphs for Open World Interactive Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05642)
*Imen Mahdi,Matteo Cassinelli,Fabien Despinoy,Tim Welschehold,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SCOUT的新方法，用于在家庭环境中进行开放世界交互式物体搜索，该方法基于3D场景图和学习到的效用函数，结合关系探索启发式策略，并通过离线程序化知识蒸馏将大语言模型中的结构化知识提取为轻量级模型，实现了高效且具备开放词汇泛化能力的实时搜索。同时提出了SymSearch基准用于评估语义推理性能，实验表明SCOUT在性能上优于基于嵌入相似性的方法，接近大语言模型水平，同时保持计算效率，并能在真实环境中有效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖视觉-语言嵌入相似性，无法可靠捕捉任务相关的语义关系，或依赖大语言模型导致速度慢、成本高，难以实现实时部署，因此需要一种既能理解物体与环境间语义关系又具备高效推理能力的方法。

Method: 提出SCOUT方法，直接在3D场景图上进行搜索，利用房间-物体包含关系和物体-物体共现等关系启发式为房间、前沿区域和物体分配效用得分；并通过离线程序化蒸馏框架，从大语言模型中提取结构化关系知识训练轻量级模型，实现高效的机器人端推理；同时构建了SymSearch符号化基准用于评估。

Result: 在符号环境和仿真环境中进行的大量评估表明，SCOUT优于基于嵌入相似性的方法，性能媲美大语言模型，同时计算效率高；真实世界实验验证了其在实际感知与导航约束下的有效性。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过结合3D场景图、关系启发式和从大语言模型蒸馏出的轻量级效用模型，在开放世界交互式物体搜索任务中实现了高效、通用且可部署的语义驱动探索，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Open-world interactive object search in household environments requires understanding semantic relationships between objects and their surrounding context to guide exploration efficiently. Prior methods either rely on vision-language embeddings similarity, which does not reliably capture task-relevant relational semantics, or large language models (LLMs), which are too slow and costly for real-time deployment. We introduce SCOUT: Scene Graph-Based Exploration with Learned Utility for Open-World Interactive Object Search, a novel method that searches directly over 3D scene graphs by assigning utility scores to rooms, frontiers, and objects using relational exploration heuristics such as room-object containment and object-object co-occurrence. To make this practical without sacrificing open-vocabulary generalization, we propose an offline procedural distillation framework that extracts structured relational knowledge from LLMs into lightweight models for on-robot inference. Furthermore, we present SymSearch, a scalable symbolic benchmark for evaluating semantic reasoning in interactive object search tasks. Extensive evaluations across symbolic and simulation environments show that SCOUT outperforms embedding similarity-based methods and matches LLM-level performance while remaining computationally efficient. Finally, real-world experiments demonstrate effective transfer to physical environments, enabling open-world interactive object search under realistic sensing and navigation constraints.

</details>


### [179] [TransMASK: Masked State Representation through Learned Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05670)
*Sagar Parekh,Preston Culbertson,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TransMASK的自监督方法，用于学习机器人任务中的状态掩码，以提取与任务相关的状态成分，提升策略在不同环境下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在新环境中执行训练时学会的任务，但环境中存在许多无关状态变量（如桌椅颜色、背景杂乱），影响策略的泛化性。目标是让机器人策略自动忽略这些无关因素。

Method: 提出TransMASK方法，通过一个可学习的掩码矩阵乘以观测状态，生成偏向于相关状态的潜在表示；该掩码与模仿学习框架结合，无需额外标签或修改损失函数，并利用策略梯度反传使掩码关注对模仿人类行为重要的状态；通过对掩码行向量归一化，使其趋向专家策略的雅可比矩阵，从而区分相关与无关状态。

Result: TransMASK能够有效识别并放大状态中与任务相关的部分，抑制无关成分，在多种模仿学习框架下提升了策略的跨环境泛化性能，且无需额外标注信息。

Conclusion: TransMASK为机器人策略学习提供了一种通用的、自监督的状态选择机制，增强了对环境变化的鲁棒性，推动了模仿学习在真实场景中的应用。

Abstract: Humans train robots to complete tasks in one environment, and expect robots to perform those same tasks in new environments. As humans, we know which aspects of the environment (i.e., the state) are relevant to the task. But there are also things that do not matter; e.g., the color of the table or the presence of clutter in the background. Ideally, the robot's policy learns to ignore these irrelevant state components. Achieving this invariance improves generalization: the robot knows not to factor irrelevant variables into its control decisions, making the policy more robust to environment changes. In this paper we therefore propose a self-supervised method to learn a mask which, when multiplied by the observed state, transforms that state into a latent representation that is biased towards relevant elements. Our method -- which we call TransMASK -- can be combined with a variety of imitation learning frameworks (such as diffusion policies) without any additional labels or alterations to the loss function. To achieve this, we recognize that the learned policy updates to better match the human's true policy. This true policy only depends on the relevant parts of the state; hence, as the gradients pass back through the learned policy and our proposed mask, they increase the value for elements that cause the robot to better imitate the human. We can therefore train TransMASK at the same time as we learn the policy. By normalizing the magnitude of each row in TransMASK, we force the mask to align with the Jacobian of the expert policy: columns that correspond to relevant states have large magnitudes, while columns for irrelevant states approach zero magnitude. We compare our approach to other methods that extract relevant states for downstream imitation learning. See our project website: https://collab.me.vt.edu/TransMASK/

</details>


### [180] [Contact-Grounded Policy: Dexterous Visuotactile Policy with Generative Contact Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05687)
*Zhengtong Xu,Yeping Wang,Ben Abbatematteo,Jom Preechayasomboon,Sonny Chan,Nick Colonnese,Amirhossein H. Memar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Contact-Grounded Policy (CGP) 实现了基于接触的灵巧操作，通过预测机器人状态和触觉反馈，并利用学习到的接触一致性映射来生成可执行控制目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现更精细、更丰富的接触式灵巧操作，需要一种能够融合多点接触信息并适应不同触觉传感器的方法。

Method: CGP 通过预测实际机器人状态和触觉反馈，并使用学习到的接触一致性映射，将这些预测转换为合规控制器可执行的目标。该方法支持密集触觉阵列和基于视觉的触觉传感器。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上通过遥操作收集演示，并在多个灵巧操作任务中对 CGP 进行了评估。

Conclusion: CGP 能有效支持多种触觉传感器，实现高精度的接触丰富型灵巧操作。

Abstract: Contact-Grounded Policy (CGP) enables fine-grained, contact-rich dexterous manipulation by grounding multi-point contacts through predicting the actual robot state and tactile feedback, and by using a learned contact-consistency mapping to convert these predictions into controller-executable targets for a compliance controller. CGP supports both dense tactile arrays and vision-based tactile sensors mounted on the hand. We collect demonstrations via teleoperation in both simulation and on a physical robot, and evaluate CGP across multiple dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Introducing the transitional autonomous vehicle lane-changing dataset: Empirical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05716)
*Abhinav Sharma,Zijun He,Danjue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了北卡罗来纳过渡性自动驾驶车辆换道（NC-tALC）数据集，包含高精度轨迹数据，用于分析过渡性自动驾驶车辆在强制换道场景中的交互行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于目前缺乏捕捉过渡性自动驾驶车辆换道行为的数据集，难以评估其与人类驾驶车辆在复杂 maneuver 中的动态交互，尤其是在强制换道情境下的安全性与稳定性。

Method: 构建了两个受控实验系列：一是tAV执行换道时与ACC车辆的交互；二是tAV作为跟随者响应其他tAV切入行为。数据以20Hz频率采集，使用厘米级RTK-GPS精度记录152次试验（72次换道、80次响应）。

Result: 获得了高质量、高分辨率的轨迹数据集，能够准确刻画tAV在换道过程中的决策与响应行为。

Conclusion: NC-tALC数据集为评估过渡性自动驾驶车辆在强制换道场景中的决策逻辑和交互动态提供了严谨的实证基础。

Abstract: Transitional autonomous vehicles (tAVs), which operate beyond SAE Level 1-2 automation but short of full autonomy, are increasingly sharing the road with human-driven vehicles (HDVs). As these systems interact during complex maneuvers such as lane changes, new patterns may emerge with implications for traffic stability and safety. Assessing these dynamics, particularly during mandatory lane changes, requires high-resolution trajectory data, yet datasets capturing tAV lane-changing behavior are scarce.
  This study introduces the North Carolina Transitional Autonomous Vehicle Lane-Changing (NC-tALC) Dataset, a high-fidelity trajectory dataset designed to characterize tAV interactions during lane-changing maneuvers. The dataset includes two controlled experimental series. In the first, tAV lane-changing experiments, a tAV executes lane changes in the presence of adaptive cruise control (ACC) equipped target vehicles, enabling analysis of lane-changing execution. In the second, tAV responding experiments, two tAVs act as followers and respond to cut-in maneuvers initiated by another tAV, enabling analysis of follower response dynamics. The dataset contains 152 trials (72 lane-changing and 80 responding trials) sampled at 20 Hz with centimeter-level RTK-GPS accuracy. The NC-tALC dataset provides a rigorous empirical foundation for evaluating tAV decision-making and interaction dynamics in controlled mandatory lane-changing scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [Environment-Aware Path Generation for Robotic Additive Manufacturing of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05748)
*Mahsa Rabiei,Reza Moini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种环境感知的在线路径生成框架（PGF），用于复杂障碍环境下机器人增材制造的结构设计，通过四种路径规划算法进行评估，并定义了关键的结构与计算性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增材制造依赖预设工具路径，难以适应动态施工环境（如地球外或障碍密集场景），缺乏在线、环境自适应的路径生成方法。

Method: 构建一个环境感知的路径生成框架（PGF），集成两种基于搜索和两种基于采样的路径规划算法，实现结构的在线设计；在周期性和随机性障碍布局下，针对封闭和开放结构，采用多种结构（如路径粗糙度、RMSE、偏差等）和计算（运行时间）指标评估性能。

Result: 在高密度障碍等最具挑战性的环境中测试了四种路径规划器的可行性；评估并比较了各项性能指标的有效性，识别出最关键的结构评价指标；确定了在复杂环境下表现最优的路径规划算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的PGF框架首次实现了面向机器人增材制造的环境感知在线路径生成，为动态和复杂环境下的自动化建造提供了可行方案，同时明确了适用于此类任务的最佳路径规划算法与核心评价指标。

Abstract: Robotic Additive Manufacturing (AM) has emerged as a scalable and customizable construction method in the last decade. However, current AM design methods rely on pre-conceived (A priori) toolpath of the structure, often developed via offline slicing software. Moreover, considering the dynamic construction environments involving obstacles on terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments, there is a need for online path generation methods. Here, an environment-aware path generation framework (PGF) is proposed for the first time in which structures are designed in an online fashion by utilizing four path planning (PP) algorithms (two search-based and two sampling-based). To evaluate the performance of the proposed PGF in different obstacle arrangements (periodic, random) for two types of structures (closed and open), structural (path roughness, turns, offset, Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), deviation) and computational (run time) performance metrics are developed. Most challenging environments (i.e., dense with high number of obstacles) are considered to saturate the feasibility limits of PP algorithms. The capability of each of the four path planners used in the PGF in finding a feasible path is assessed. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed structural performance metrics is evaluated individually and comparatively, and most essential metrics necessary for evaluation of toolpath of the resulting structures are prescribed. Consequently, the most promising path planners in challenging environments are identified for robotic additive manufacturing applications.

</details>


### [183] [Vision-Language System using Open-Source LLMs for Gestures in Medical Interpreter Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05751)
*Thanh-Tung Ngo,Emma Murphy,Robert J. Ross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种隐私保护的视觉-语言框架，用于医疗翻译机器人检测言语行为并生成相应的机器人手势，基于本地部署的开源模型，利用大语言模型进行意图检测，并引入了标注临床对话和配对动作片段的新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗环境中，跨语言障碍的有效沟通至关重要，非语言线索和手势起着关键作用。需要一种能够理解言语行为并生成合适手势的医疗翻译机器人系统。

Method: 采用本地部署的开源模型，结合大语言模型与少样本提示进行意图检测，构建了一个包含言语行为标注和对应手势视频的数据集，并开发了识别模块和手势生成模块。

Result: 识别模块达到了0.90的准确率、0.93的加权精确率和0.91的加权F1分数；用户研究表明，该方法在类人性方面优于基线模型，同时保持相当的适当性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在保护隐私的前提下，提升了医疗翻译机器人的沟通效率和自然性，具有较高的应用潜力。

Abstract: Effective communication is vital in healthcare, especially across language barriers, where non-verbal cues and gestures are critical. This paper presents a privacy-preserving vision-language framework for medical interpreter robots that detects specific speech acts (consent and instruction) and generates corresponding robotic gestures. Built on locally deployed open-source models, the system utilizes a Large Language Model (LLM) with few-shot prompting for intent detection. We also introduce a novel dataset of clinical conversations annotated for speech acts and paired with gesture clips. Our identification module achieved 0.90 accuracy, 0.93 weighted precision, and a 0.91 weighted F1-Score. Our approach significantly improves computational efficiency and, in user studies, outperforms the speech-gesture generation baseline in human-likeness while maintaining comparable appropriateness.

</details>


### [184] [EmboAlign: Aligning Video Generation with Compositional Constraints for Zero-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05757)
*Gehao Zhang,Zhenyang Ni,Payal Mohapatra,Han Liu,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练数据的框架\method{}，通过结合视觉-语言模型（VLM）的结构化空间推理能力，在推理时对视频生成模型（VGM）的输出进行约束引导和轨迹优化，显著提升零样本机器人操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型（VGM）虽能生成连贯的视频，但常产生物理上不合理的动作，且在将像素运动转化为机器人动作时因深度估计和关键点跟踪不准确而引入累积误差。因此需要一种方法来提升VGM在零样本机器人操作中的物理合理性和执行精度。

Method: \method{}利用VLM从语言指令中自动提取任务相关的组合式约束，并将这些约束应用于两个阶段：（1）约束引导的 rollout 选择，对多个VGM生成的视频序列打分筛选，保留最符合物理规律的候选；（2）基于约束的轨迹优化，以选中的序列为初始值，进一步优化机器人轨迹以纠正重定向误差。

Result: 在六个需要精确执行的真实机器人操作任务上进行评估，\method{}相比最强基线平均成功率提升了43.3个百分点，且无需任何任务特定的训练数据。

Conclusion: \method{}通过融合VLM的结构化推理与VGM的动态生成能力，实现了更安全、更精准的零样本机器人操作，展示了语言模型在指导生成模型满足物理与任务约束方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Video generative models (VGMs) pretrained on large-scale internet data can produce temporally coherent rollout videos that capture rich object dynamics, offering a compelling foundation for zero-shot robotic manipulation. However, VGMs often produce physically implausible rollouts, and converting their pixel-space motion into robot actions through geometric retargeting further introduces cumulative errors from imperfect depth estimation and keypoint tracking. To address these challenges, we present \method{}, a data-free framework that aligns VGM outputs with compositional constraints generated by vision-language models (VLMs) at inference time. The key insight is that VLMs offer a capability complementary to VGMs: structured spatial reasoning that can identify the physical constraints critical to the success and safety of manipulation execution. Given a language instruction, \method{} uses a VLM to automatically extract a set of compositional constraints capturing task-specific requirements, which are then applied at two stages: (1) constraint-guided rollout selection, which scores and filters a batch of VGM rollouts to retain the most physically plausible candidate, and (2) constraint-based trajectory optimization, which uses the selected rollout as initialization and refines the robot trajectory under the same constraint set to correct retargeting errors. We evaluate \method{} on six real-robot manipulation tasks requiring precise, constraint-sensitive execution, improving the overall success rate by 43.3\% points over the strongest baseline without any task-specific training data.

</details>


### [185] [Multi-Robot Trajectory Planning via Constrained Bayesian Optimization and Local Cost Map Learning with STL-Based Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05767)
*Sourav Raxit,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Jose Fuentes,Paulo Padrao,Ana Cavalcanti,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于满足信号时序逻辑（STL）规范和动力学约束的多机器人运动规划的两阶段框架，结合了基于采样的在线学习与形式化STL推理，在单机器人和多机器人层面分别设计了cBOT和STL-KCBS算法，提升了轨迹效率、安全性与可扩展性，并通过仿真与实物实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确方法在多机器人STL运动规划中面临可扩展性差和适应性不足的问题，而传统基于采样的方法需要大量采样才能生成最优轨迹，难以兼顾安全性、实时性与形式化规范满足。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：在单机器人层面采用基于约束贝叶斯优化的树搜索（cBOT）方法，利用高斯过程学习局部代价图与可行性约束；在多机器人层面设计STL增强的动力学冲突基于搜索（STL-KCBS）算法，将STL监控融入冲突检测与解决过程，确保满足任务规范并保持可扩展性与概率完备性。

Result: 基准测试显示所提方法在轨迹效率与安全性上优于现有方法，真实无人水面艇实验验证了其在不确定环境中的鲁棒性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效结合了数据驱动学习与形式化推理，解决了多机器人系统在复杂动态环境中满足高级任务规范的运动规划难题，具备良好的可扩展性、安全性与实际应用前景。

Abstract: We address multi-robot motion planning under Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications with kinodynamic constraints. Exact approaches face scalability bottlenecks and limited adaptability, while conventional sampling-based methods require excessive samples to construct optimal trajectories. We propose a two-stage framework integrating sampling-based online learning with formal STL reasoning. At the single-robot level, our constrained Bayesian Optimization-based Tree search (cBOT) planner uses a Gaussian process as a surrogate model to learn local cost maps and feasibility constraints, generating shorter collision-free trajectories with fewer samples. At the multi-robot level, our STL-enhanced Kinodynamic Conflict-Based Search (STL-KCBS) algorithm incorporates STL monitoring into conflict detection and resolution, ensuring specification satisfaction while maintaining scalability and probabilistic completeness. Benchmarking demonstrates improved trajectory efficiency and safety over existing methods. Real-world experiments with autonomous surface vehicles validate robustness and practical applicability in uncertain environments. The STLcBOT Planner will be released as an open-source package, and videos of real-world and simulated experiments are available at https://stlbot.github.io/.

</details>


### [186] [Task-Level Decisions to Gait Level Control: A Hierarchical Policy Approach for Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05783)
*Sijia Li,Haoyu Wang,Shenghai Yuan,Yizhuo Yang,Thien-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为任务级决策到步态控制（TDGC）的分层策略架构，用于四足机器人导航，通过高低层策略协同实现鲁棒且可解释的跨地形运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的四足导航面临高层决策与底层步态执行之间的尺度不匹配问题，以及在分布外环境变化下的不稳定性挑战，传统端到端方法难以适应且缺乏可调接口。

Method: 设计了一个分层策略架构：低层策略在仿真中通过强化学习训练，实现步态条件下的运动控制，并将任务需求映射为可调控的行为参数；高层策略基于稀疏语义或几何地形线索做出任务级决策并转化为低层目标；引入基于性能驱动的结构化课程学习来逐步增加环境难度和扰动范围。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在混合地形和分布外测试中实现了更高的任务成功率，具备更强的适应性和稳定性。

Conclusion: TDGC架构通过显式的接口支持部署时调优、故障诊断和策略精炼，提升了四足机器人在复杂真实环境中的导航能力与可维护性。

Abstract: Real-world quadruped navigation is constrained by a scale mismatch between high-level navigation decisions and low-level gait execution, as well as by instabilities under out-of-distribution environmental changes. Such variations challenge sim-to-real transfer and can trigger falls when policies lack explicit interfaces for adaptation. In this paper, we present a hierarchical policy architecture for quadrupedal navigation, termed Task-level Decision to Gait Control (TDGC). A low-level policy, trained with reinforcement learning in simulation, delivers gait-conditioned locomotion and maps task requirements to a compact set of controllable behavior parameters, enabling robust mode generation and smooth switching. A high-level policy makes task-centric decisions from sparse semantic or geometric terrain cues and translates them into low-level targets, forming a traceable decision pipeline without dense maps or high-resolution terrain reconstruction. Different from end-to-end approaches, our architecture provides explicit interfaces for deployment-time tuning, fault diagnosis, and policy refinement. We introduce a structured curriculum with performance-driven progression that expands environmental difficulty and disturbance ranges. Experiments show higher task success rates on mixed terrains and out-of-distribution tests.

</details>


### [187] [CDF-Glove: A Cable-Driven Force Feedback Glove for Dexterous Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05804)
*Huayue Liang,Ruochong Li,Yaodong Yang,Long Zeng,Yuanpei Chen,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、轻量化的力反馈手套CDF-Glove，用于提升灵巧操作中遥操作演示的质量，从而改进模仿学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的遥操作演示在灵巧操作的模仿学习中是主要瓶颈，现有平台通常缺乏触觉反馈且昂贵笨重。

Method: 设计了具有20个手指自由度实时状态感知的CDF-Glove，包含运动学模型与控制架构，并在多种机器人手上验证；引入力反馈以提高操作精度。

Result: CDF-Glove实现远端关节重复精度0.4度，力反馈延迟约200ms，任务成功率相比无反馈提升4倍；基于其收集的数据训练的策略比示教方法成功率提高55%，完成时间减少15.2秒（相对降低47.2%）；成本约为230美元。

Conclusion: CDF-Glove是一种低成本、高性能的力反馈遥操作解决方案，显著提升了模仿学习中遥操作演示的质量和策略学习效果。

Abstract: High-quality teleoperated demonstrations are a primary bottleneck for imitation learning (IL) in dexterous manipulation. However, haptic feedback provides operators with real-time contact information, enabling real-time finger posture adjustments, and thereby improving demonstration quality. Existing dexterous teleoperation platforms typically omit haptic feedback and remain bulky and expensive. We introduce CDF-Glove, a lightweight and low cost cable-driven force-feedback glove. The real-time state is available for 20 finger degrees of freedom (DoF), of which 16 are directly sensed and 4 are passively coupled (inferred from kinematic constraints). We develop a kinematic model and control stack for the glove, and validate them across multiple robotic hands with diverse kinematics and DoF. The CDF-Glove achieves distal joint repeatability of 0.4 degrees, and delivers about 200 ms force feedback latency, yielding a 4x improvement in task success rate relative to no-feedback teleoperation. We collect two bimanual teleoperation datasets, on which we train and evaluate Diffusion Policy baselines. Compared to kinesthetic teaching, the policies trained in our teleoperated demonstrations increase the average success rate by 55% and reduce the mean completion time by approximately 15.2 seconds (a 47.2% relative reduction). In particular, the CDF-Glove costs approximately US$230. The code and designs are released as open source at https://cdfglove.github.io/.

</details>


### [188] [Hierarchical Latent Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05815)
*Hanjung Kim,Lerrel Pinto,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiLAM是一种分层潜在动作模型，通过建模长期时序信息来发现视频中的高层次潜在技能，利用预训练的低层级LAM提取动态模式序列，并在实验中展现出优于基线的动态技能发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAMs）主要关注短时间帧间转换，捕捉低层次运动，忽略了无动作视频中常见的长时间、高层次技能结构，因此需要一种能建模长期时序依赖的方法来发现高阶技能。

Method: 提出HiLAM，采用分层架构，利用预训练的LAM作为低级特征提取器，聚合潜在动作序列为高级潜在技能，从而建模长时程依赖关系。

Result: 实验证明HiLAM在技能发现任务上优于基线模型，具备更强的长期动态模式捕捉能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HiLAM通过分层建模有效发现了无动作视频中的高层次技能，为从无标注视频中学习长期行为结构提供了新思路。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) enable learning from actionless data for applications ranging from robotic control to interactive world models. However, existing LAMs typically focus on short-horizon frame transitions and capture low-level motion while overlooking longer-term temporal structure. In contrast, actionless videos often contain temporally extended and high-level skills. We present HiLAM, a hierarchical latent action model that discovers latent skills by modeling long-term temporal information. To capture these dependencies across long horizons, we utilize a pretrained LAM as a low-level extractor. This architecture aggregates latent action sequences, which contain the underlying dynamic patterns of the video, into high-level latent skills. Our experiments demonstrate that HiLAM improves over the baseline and exhibits robust dynamic skill discovery.

</details>


### [189] [OpenHEART: Opening Heterogeneous Articulated Objects with a Legged Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05830)
*Seonghyeon Lim,Hyeonwoo Lee,Seunghyun Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于足式机械臂开启异构铰接物体的鲁棒且采样高效的框架，通过SAFE和ArtIEst方法提升了跨域泛化能力和感知融合效果，并在仿真与真实机器人系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于物体铰接类型的多样性和足式机器人复杂的动力学特性，现有基于强化学习的方法依赖高维感知输入，导致采样效率低，难以实现对异构铰接物体的稳健交互。

Method: 提出了Sampling-based Abstracted Feature Extraction (SAFE) 方法，将手柄和面板几何信息编码为低维紧凑表示，并引入Articulation Information Estimator (ArtIEst) 自适应融合本体感知与外部感知，估计物体的开启方向和运动范围。

Result: 所提框架在仿真和真实机器人系统中成功实现了对多种异构铰接物体（如门、抽屉、柜子）的操作，表现出良好的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAFE与ArtIEst相结合显著提高了采样效率和跨域操作能力，为足式机械臂在复杂环境中操作多样化铰接物体提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Legged manipulators offer high mobility and versatile manipulation. However, robust interaction with heterogeneous articulated objects, such as doors, drawers, and cabinets, remains challenging because of the diverse articulation types of the objects and the complex dynamics of the legged robot. Existing reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches often rely on high-dimensional sensory inputs, leading to sample inefficiency. In this paper, we propose a robust and sample-efficient framework for opening heterogeneous articulated objects with a legged manipulator. In particular, we propose Sampling-based Abstracted Feature Extraction (SAFE), which encodes handle and panel geometry into a compact low-dimensional representation, improving cross-domain generalization. Additionally, Articulation Information Estimator (ArtIEst) is introduced to adaptively mix proprioception with exteroception to estimate opening direction and range of motion for each object. The proposed framework was deployed to manipulate various heterogeneous articulated objects in simulation and real-world robot systems. Videos can be found on the project website: https://openheart-icra.github.io/OpenHEART/

</details>


### [190] [Terrain characterization and locomotion adaptation in a small-scale lizard-inspired robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05837)
*Duncan Andrews,Landon Zimmerman,Evan Martin,Joe DiGennaro,Baxi Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种小型智能蜥蜴启发的自适应机器人（SILA Bot），通过本体感觉信号估计颗粒介质深度，并采用线性反馈控制策略实现对复杂地形的有效自适应运动，为小尺度机器人在未知环境中的低计算复杂度感知与控制提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺度机器人在现实环境中应用受限，主要由于其与地形的相互作用随尺寸减小而发生根本变化，且缺乏对如何感知和响应复杂自然地形的系统理解。

Method: 设计了一种受蜥蜴启发的小型自适应机器人（SILA Bot），使用不同深度的颗粒介质作为代表性地形，利用关节力矩等本体感觉信号通过K近邻分类器估计颗粒深度，并基于深度与身体波动模式之间的线性关系设计线性反馈控制器调节身体相位。

Result: 实现了95%准确率的颗粒深度估计，发现最优身体运动模式可由颗粒深度线性参数化，所提出的反馈控制显著提升了机器人在未知深度地形上的运动性能。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一个适用于小尺度机器人的感知与控制原则框架，能够在保持低计算复杂度的同时实现有效的地形自适应运动。

Abstract: Unlike their large-scale counterparts, small-scale robots are largely confined to laboratory environments and are rarely deployed in real-world settings. As robot size decreases, robot-terrain interactions fundamentally change; however, there remains a lack of systematic understanding of what sensory information small-scale robots should acquire and how they should respond when traversing complex natural terrains. To address these challenges, we develop a Small-scale, Intelligent, Lizard-inspired, Adaptive Robot (SILA Bot) capable of adapting to diverse substrates. We use granular media of varying depths as a controlled yet representative terrain paradigm. We show that the optimal body movement pattern (ranging from standing-wave bending that assists limb retraction on flat ground to traveling-wave undulation that generates thrust in deep granular media) can be parameterized and approximated as a linear function of granular depth. Furthermore, proprioceptive signals, such as joint torque, provide sufficient information to estimate granular depth via a K-Nearest Neighbors classifier, achieving 95% accuracy. Leveraging these relationships, we design a simple linear feedback controller that modulates body phase and substantially improves locomotion performance on terrains with unknown depth.
